## Bound outside the measured library

Bindings of other modules (QtOpenGL, QtOpenGLWidgets, QtMultimedia, QtMultimediaWidgets), of deprecated members, of signals' C++ names, and the extension's own glue; none of them is counted.

- `QAudioOutput::__construct`
